# Runtime Data Ledger rev 3 - group render

Part of the [runtime data ledger, rev 3](../RUNTIME-DATA-LEDGER.md) (the index: vocabularies, totals, entity model, kernel features, decisions). **Tree:** `D:/wt/joltab`, `merge/ke16-into-ecsnative` @ `d11962a9`. **Rows:** 169, of which **160 active** and **9 SUPERSEDED** by the ui-lane (kept below, marked, not counted, not in the TSV); 322 non_rows (kept in the group JSON ledger).

Active forms: diagnostics 75, system-scratch 50, resource-column 16, enable-state 8, dense-component 6, kernel-internal 3, out-of-scope:test-only 2. Active classes: K 1, R 6, F 5, B 66, D 80, C 2. Crates: boyko_render 160. Rungs: OOS 2, R2 1, R4 11, R6 146.

## Contents

- [Findings](#findings)
- [Kernel features](#kernel-features)
- [Primitives refuted](#primitives-refuted)
- [Plan conflicts](#plan-conflicts)
- [Rows](#rows)

## Findings

- LATENT DEFECT: a device-memory leak. The only production `Assets::fill` caller discards the rejected value (gpu_upload.rs:120 "let _ = assets.fill(staged.handle, gpu);"), and MeshGpu's drop glue frees no device memory (mesh.rs:204 "trivial drop), freeing NO device memory."). A fill-reject therefore leaks device buffers, and for textures a bindless slot as well. The Orphaned*Gpu queues built for this case have no producer. KF-37 fixes it. How often a reject can happen was not assessed. **[rev 2]** Verdict in the index (Defects, B): CONFIRMED in code, latent in practice (the drain runs once, at boot). Fix now with the existing Orphaned*Gpu queues; KF-37 is superseded by the engine's K6'.
- Dead in the shipped binary: load_material_folder (texture.rs:901; only boyko_app tests call it), the barrier plan, GpuColumnManager.meta and the UI pack/upload path. Their forms are design decisions, not live-path decisions.
- **[rev 3]** The 8 joltab rows in `src/ui` and `bindless.rs:387` are SUPERSEDED by the ui-lane, which rewrote those files (item 1). The asset rows follow engine Q1 (item 2).

The features this group proposed, with the index id they merged into.

| group name | index id | rev-3 status |
|---|---|---|
| KF-owning-scratch-column | KF-02 | active |
| KF-assets-adopt-retiring | KF-37 | superseded |
| KF-assets-iter-mut | KF-38 | withdrawn |
| KF-enable-initial-polarity | KF-17 | active |
| KF-loader-decode-context | KF-09 | active |
| KF-structured-asset-error | KF-10 | active |
| KF-device-column-residency (existing seam, ARCH-AUDIT Stage 5) | KF-40 | active |

## Primitives refuted

- **pub VmColumn&lt;T: Copy> (visibility change)**
  - replaced by: resource-column on the existing pub ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> (ComponentPool-backed) owned by the Resource; GpuColumnMeta goes kernel-internal instead
  - evidence: boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/scratch/scratch_column.rs:43 "pub struct ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> {"; boyko_render/src/light_system.rs:90 "scratch: ScratchColumn&lt;u8>," (render already keeps a durable ScratchColumn in a Send Resource); boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/backing.rs:115 "pub fn register_asset_layout&lt;T: 'static>(drop_fn: Option&lt;DropFn>) -> ComponentId {" (any layout, so 16 B (u32, u64) rows need no VmColumn change); D:/wt/joltab/docs/ARCH-AUDIT-ECS-DATA-REMEDIATION.md:19 "For non-entity bulk: a `Resource` that OWNS a `ComponentPool` host column."
  - note: ScratchColumn's build view (push / truncate / as_mut_slice / extend_from_slice) covers a stack and a swap_remove list. Exporting VmColumn would add a second typed column beside ComponentPool.
  - rows: bindless.rs:73; bindless.rs:74; gpu_column.rs:532 (goes kernel-internal)
- **VmQueue&lt;T> (owning move-out queue for non-Copy values)**
  - replaced by: (a) orphans: Retiring rows of the SAME Assets&lt;T> store (resource-column) via KF-assets-adopt-retiring; (b) retired buffers/TLASes: a NonSend Resource owning a ComponentPool column via KF-owning-scratch-column
  - evidence: boyko_render/src/mesh.rs:231 "register_asset_layout::&lt;MeshGpu>(Some(MeshGpu::drop_glue))" (non-Copy asset values already live in a pool with drop glue); boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/assets.rs:998 "pub fn retire(&mut self, slot: u32) -> Option&lt;T> {" (the fence-gated move-out already exists); boyko_ecs/src/ecs/memory/component_pool.rs:1694 "pub(crate) unsafe fn take_at&lt;T: 'static>(&mut self, idx: usize) -> T {"
  - note: Neither needs a storage primitive; what is missing is a typed API over ComponentPool.
  - rows: mesh_assets.rs:712; texture.rs:928; retired_gpu_buffers.rs:53; retired_gpu_buffers.rs:59
- **VmSlice&lt;T: Copy> (exact-size immutable Send slab)**
  - replaced by: resource-column: a contiguous range of a Resource-owned ScratchColumn (CSR: flat rows plus (start, len)); for Asset::Cpu the Cpu value becomes the Copy range record, which is Send
  - evidence: boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/asset.rs:34 "type Cpu: Send + 'static;"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/server.rs:133 "staging.push(Staged { handle, cpu });" (decode is synchronous on the caller thread); boyko_render/src/barrier.rs:59 "#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]"
  - rows: gpu_system.rs:155/181/242; barrier.rs:240; mesh_data.rs:28/30; texture_data.rs:28; loaders/glb.rs:915/916; loaders/obj.rs:183/187/188; loaders/png_texture.rs:42/50
- **LoadArena (+ ArenaVec, ArenaStr)**
  - replaced by: system-scratch: ScratchColumn lanes of the loader decode context (KF-loader-decode-context). The one non-Copy loader structure, the glb Json tree, reshapes into a flat Copy tape; &str tokens become (start, len) offsets; the barrier routing list flattens into the Copy PlannedBarrier lane (resource-column)
  - evidence: boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:90 "enum Json {" (the only recursive non-Copy transient); every other loader transient element is Copy: f32, usize, bool, (usize, Mat4), Vertex, u32, Vec3, [f32; 3], [f32; 2], CornerRecord, Corner; boyko_render/src/barrier.rs:59 "#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]" (the barrier element is Copy once the Box is flattened away)
  - rows: loaders/glb.rs Json/Parser/resolve_instance_transform locals; loaders/obj.rs decode locals; tangent.rs:62-63; texture.rs:806/808/828; barrier.rs:208/213/241
- **InlineStr&lt;N>**
  - replaced by: kernel diagnostics substrate: a structured AssetError (code + POD args) emitted by the existing boyko_log call (KF-structured-asset-error); the SHA-256 digest becomes [u8; 64]; the glb primitive label becomes two integers
  - evidence: boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/server.rs:171 "boyko_log::codes::E0801," (the only production reader of the String); D:/wt/joltab/docs/LOGGING-SYSTEM-PLAN.md:150 "### Decision 1: Deferred formatting"; boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:923 "let what = format!("mesh {mesh_index} primitive {pi}");"
  - rows: all 73 loader D rows; loaders/glb.rs:923; vg_census.rs:265/279
- **InlineVec&lt;T, N>**
  - replaced by: plain fixed-size Rust arrays ([u64; 64], [u32; 256] + len, [UiRingSlot; FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT] built in place)
  - evidence: boyko_render/src/ui/resources.rs:305 "let slots: [UiRingSlot; FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT] = built"
  - note: No primitive is needed: the bounded inline form is the language's array type.
  - rows: vg_census.rs:54/66/134/137; ui/resources.rs:275
- **ScratchOut&lt;T> (kernel system-output mode)**
  - replaced by: enable-state: LightEnabled reads ENABLED by default (KF-enable-initial-polarity), which removes the id-collecting systems entirely, so no system has a row-list output
  - evidence: boyko_render/src/light.rs:244 "/// A never-toggled row reads DISABLED (the bitset default). To keep pre-existing /"; boyko_render/src/light.rs:253 "#[component(storage = "bitset")]"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/enable/enable_store.rs:204 "4096-row range) reads as `false` (all-disabled). Hot read path."
  - note: If the owner rejects a default-on polarity, the fallback is to expose entity ids on the exclusive QueryView, which is still not ScratchOut.
  - rows: light_system.rs:729/733/736/739/742/745/748/751

## Plan conflicts

- D:/wt/joltab/docs/ASSET-STREAMING-PLAN.md:327 "Fill-reject orphans (rejected `MeshGpu` with live buffers) route through a `!Send OrphanedMeshGpu` queue on the" and D:/wt/joltab/docs/ASSET-STREAMING-PLAN.md:377 "old buffers ride a NEW `RetiredGpuBuffers` (`!Send Vec` teardown queue mirroring `OrphanedMeshGpu`" chose dedicated !Send Vec queues for OrphanedMeshGpu (and its texture sibling) and RetiredGpuBuffers. This ledger routes orphans into Assets&lt;T> Retiring rows and retired buffers into a Resource-owned ComponentPool column. The plan predates the 2026-09-10 order; the conflict is recorded here, not silently overridden.
- D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:358 "\| render/rhi persistent lists (`bindless.rs`, `retired_gpu_buffers.rs`, `gpu_column.rs:532`, FrameGraph SoA lanes) \| `HeapVec` / `VmColumn` (12-24 B tuples now admitted) \|": this ledger instead sends bindless to ScratchColumn resource-columns, retired buffers to KF-owning-scratch-column, and gpu_column.rs:532 to the kernel's DeviceColumn record.
- D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:343 "render `ui/pack.rs:143-150` \| `FrameVec` on the `FrameArena`"; D:/wt/joltab/docs/MEMORY-SYSTEM-AUDIT.md:137 "\| UiRenderScratch (pack + keys) \|" calls the same Vecs OK-by-design. This ledger makes UiInstance a component on the widget, the key lane a system ScratchColumn and the gather target the mapped ring. That is three positions, and none is silently picked.
- D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:361 "`mesh_data.rs`, `texture_data.rs`" sends mesh_data.rs / texture_data.rs to HeapVec; this ledger sends the payload to staging resource-column lanes. The two AGREE on errors (D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:361 "error types carry codes (`boyko_log/src/codes.rs` already exists) + `&'static str`").
- D:/wt/joltab/docs/MEMORY-SYSTEM-AUDIT.md:124 "\| GpuColumnManager.{registry,meta,staging} \|" (OK-by-design, restated by D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-RESEARCH.md:663 "(audit `:124` OK-by-design)") against D:/wt/joltab/docs/ARCH-AUDIT-ECS-DATA-REMEDIATION.md:31 "- **Stage 5 (S4):** fold `GpuColumnManager.meta` into per-column archetype metadata (`PoolBacking::Device` arm). Cold; lowest urgency.". The two plans disagree; this ledger follows Stage 5.
- D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md:111 "`ScratchColumn` + new `DropColumn<T>`": the same capability as KF-owning-scratch-column, but homed as a new column primitive on VmReservation instead of a typed view over ComponentPool. The owner already rejected a bespoke VmReservation-backed column beside ComponentPool for physics.

## Rows

Every row, no elision, sorted by file and line. Columns:

- **file:line + evidence:** the evidence is the verbatim source line in `D:/wt/joltab`.
- **owner**, **container&lt;elem&gt;**, **class**, **ecs_form**, **owning_entity**
- **memory destination:** the recount's. A `NEW:` name here was refuted in the index (Primitives refuted) unless it is KEPT there.
- **plan_ref**
- **note:** `[kind; growth; addr_cached; rung]`, then `ECS:` with the form rationale (one sentence per skipped earlier form, then any `[rev2 ...]` blocks), then `Class:` with the inventory/recount note, then `Readers:`.

| crates/boyko_render/src/asset_refcount.rs:556 `scratch: &mut Vec<FreeEntry>,` | retire_deferred_frees(scratch) - backing field is boyko_app HostState::retire_scratch (boyko_app/src/host.rs:96) | `Vec<FreeEntry>` | F | dense-component | asset | ScratchColumn |  | [param; growth highwater; addr_cached no; rung R4] ECS: The datum is a copy of the ready rows of DeferredFree, the durable fence-gated free queue (boyko_scene/src/asset_refs.rs:149 "entries: Vec&lt;FreeEntry>,", boyko_scene group). component: an asset row is not an entity - Assets&lt;T> is a Resource-owned standalone pool (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/assets.rs:201 "col: ComponentPool,"), so there is no entity row to carry it. dense-component: same reason - a dense component is still one row per entity. enable-state: the datum is a payload, not a per-entity boolean. relation: no edge between two entities. event: produced and consumed inside one call by one pass, not a message between systems. => resource-column: the rows are read IN PLACE in DeferredFree's own kernel column and the copy disappears. The ready rows are a FIFO prefix: the only production push stamps a monotone epoch (boyko_render/src/asset_refcount.rs:185 "free.push(FreeEntry { kind, slot, retire_frame: epoch + RETIRE_DELAY });"), so the pass reads the prefix with retire_frame &lt;= epoch and advances a head index. The copy exists today only because boyko_render/src/asset_refcount.rs:552 "pub fn retire_deferred_frees(" is a host call holding &mut EcsMaster, which cannot borrow DeferredFree and Assets&lt;MeshGpu> at once (boyko_render/src/asset_refcount.rs:590 "world.resource_mut::&lt;DeferredFree>().drain_ready(epoch, scratch);"). As a scheduled system with ResMut&lt;DeferredFree> plus NonSendResMut&lt;Assets&lt;..>> params, both borrows are live together. Fallback if the pass stays a host call: system-scratch, a ScratchColumn&lt;FreeEntry> (boyko_scene/src/asset_refs.rs:130 "pub struct FreeEntry {" is Copy). [rev3 item 2, engine decision Q1 applied] main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DECISIONS.md:23 "\| Q1 \| Assets as entities \| **(a) Assets are entities** \| yes \| A per-draw handle-resolution microbench regressing". The rev-2 skip reason "an asset row is not an entity" is withdrawn: under Q1 every asset is an entity. ecs_form dense-component: the datum is a copy of the ready retire entries, and under Q1 those are the `dying` slots of each asset K3 group, stamped with their retire tick; the release pass reads them in place and frees at the horizon (KF-49: main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:649 "\| `DeferredFree` **(rev 2)** \| Resource Vec \| deleted: K3 `dying` + K6′ horizon (ED16) \| AS2 \|"), so the copy and DeferredFree both disappear. owning_entity asset: the pass spans every asset kind. Class: FreeEntry is Copy POD (boyko_scene/src/asset_refs.rs:129-130), so ScratchColumn&lt;FreeEntry> fits as-is. The owning field is in the boyko_app group; this row is the render-side signature that forces it to be a std Vec. Readers: retire_deferred_frees drains DeferredFree::drain_ready(epoch, scratch) into it each frame (O(1) early-out when every retire queue is empty), then iterates it by AssetRefKind; called from boyko_app/src/runner.rs:1264 per frame and :3507 at teardown (with a fresh Vec::new()) |

B by lifetime, data by use. component/dense-component: systems are not entities in this kernel (the Schedule owns them), so a per-system datum has no entity row. enable-state: the datum is a payload, not a per-entity boolean. relation: the conflict EDGES are the input, but the lowered plan is per-consumer data, not an edge between entities. event: it is durable from schedule build onward. => resource-column: a render GpuBarrierPlan Resource owning one flat ScratchColumn&lt;PlannedBarrier> (boyko_render/src/barrier.rs:59 "#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]", repr(C) Copy) grouped by consumer, plus a ScratchColumn&lt;(u32 consumer, u32 start, u32 len)> range lane; each GpuSystem keeps its (start, len) instead of a Box&lt;[PlannedBarrier]>. This refutes the recount's LoadArena destination: flattening removes the non-Copy (u32, Box&lt;[PlannedBarrier]>) element. kernel-internal (a Schedule-owned build product, boyko_render/src/gpu_system.rs:236 "A full Schedule-internal auto-wire is a") is refuted today by the dependency direction: PlannedBarrier holds boyko_rhi stage/access types (boyko_rhi/src/enums.rs:115 "pub struct BarrierStage(u32);") and boyko_ecs does not depend on boyko_rhi (boyko_ecs/Cargo.toml:7 "boyko-utils =  { path = "../boyko_utils" }" is its first dep line; no boyko_rhi line). The kernel already carries the abstract intent on SystemMeta (boyko_render/src/gpu_system.rs:187 "meta.set_gpu_intent(intent.clone());"), so moving the LOWERING into the kernel would require an abstract barrier vocabulary there - recorded, not decided. NO production caller: lower_barriers, GpuSystem::new and set_barriers are reached only from boyko_render/tests and barrier.rs's own test module. This row is the durable GpuSystem field or its params; it becomes a (start, len) range. Class: Lowered once at schedule build, immutable afterwards, read every frame. B by lifetime (built once at setup) although read on the frame path; an exact-size VM-backed immutable slab is the VM-native Box&lt;[T]>. Readers: GpuSystem::run_dispatcher replays barriers() before each compute dispatch (per frame, read-only); GpuSystem::new / set_barriers have NO production caller in this tree (only boyko_render/tests/zero_readback.rs:115/137 and tests/sync_validation.rs) |

| crates/boyko_render/src/gpu_upload.rs:216 `let mut pending: Vec<Handle<MeshGpu>> = Vec::with_capacity(assets.len());` | backfill_vb_geometry_slots::pending | `Vec<Handle<MeshGpu>>` | B | dense-component | mesh | ScratchColumn |  | [local; growth once; addr_cached no; rung R6] ECS: A handle list collected only because Assets&lt;T> has a borrowed iter() (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/assets.rs:703 "pub fn iter(&self) -> impl Iterator&lt;Item = (Handle&lt;T>, &T)> + '_ {") and no iter_mut or owned drain (boyko_render/src/mesh_assets.rs:577 "// `Assets<T>` exposes no owned/mutable whole-table iteration (only the"). The datum is the set of live rows of the store itself. component: an asset row is not an entity - Assets&lt;T> is a Resource-owned standalone pool (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/assets.rs:201 "col: ComponentPool,"), so there is no entity row to carry it. dense-component: same reason - a dense component is still one row per entity. enable-state: the predicate (reserved geometry slot / live) is read off the row, not stored. relation: no edge between two entities. event: produced and consumed inside one call by one pass, not a message between systems. => resource-column: iterate the store's own ComponentPool rows in place (kernel feature KF-assets-iter-mut); the handle list disappears. Boot one-shot (backfill_vb_geometry_slots). [rev3 item 2, engine decision Q1 applied] main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DECISIONS.md:23 "\| Q1 \| Assets as entities \| **(a) Assets are entities** \| yes \| A per-draw handle-resolution microbench regressing". The rev-2 skip reason "an asset row is not an entity" is withdrawn: under Q1 every asset is an entity. ecs_form dense-component: the store is the asset kind's K3 dense group with RELEASE = Deferred (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:216 "- **Value and release.** The asset value is a column of a **K3 dense group with `RELEASE = Deferred`** on the asset entity (physics K3/D14,"), so this handle list disappears into an in-place walk of the group column (KF-19); no Assets&lt;T> iteration API is needed (KF-38 stays withdrawn). Class: Exists only because Assets&lt;T> exposes no mutable whole-table iteration; an iter_mut on the (other-group) Assets store would remove it. Readers: backfill_vb_geometry_slots - boot one-shot called by boyko_app::runner right after upload_mesh_assets |
| crates/boyko_render/src/light_system.rs:729 `q.iter_entities().map(\|(id, _)\| id).collect::<Vec<_>>()` | light_seed_state -> LightSeedState::added_dir closure (System::Out = Vec&lt;EntityId>) | `Vec<EntityId>` | F | enable-state | light | NEW:ScratchOut&lt;T> | D:/wt/joltab/docs/CAPABILITY-STATE-MODEL.md:67 "\| **Lighting** \| light-component presence \|" | [local; growth perframe; addr_cached no; rung R4] ECS: Eight cached id systems return Vec&lt;EntityId> (boyko_render/src/light_system.rs:717 "impl System&lt;Out = Vec&lt;EntityId>>,") and exist only to find lights whose LightEnabled bit was never set, because boyko_render/src/light.rs:244 "/// A never-toggled row reads DISABLED (the bitset default). To keep pre-existing /". component/dense-component: the datum is a list of light ids, not a light's payload; what it serves is one boolean per light. => enable-state: LightEnabled is already an EnableTag (boyko_render/src/light.rs:253 "#[component(storage = "bitset")]"). If a light row reads ENABLED until disabled, the whole seed pass disappears: the Added&lt;> scans, the id lists, the ScratchColumn&lt;EntityId> (boyko_render/src/light_system.rs:702 "ids: ScratchColumn&lt;EntityId>,") and the System::Out = Vec contract. The cause is the store's fixed polarity (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/enable/enable_store.rs:204 "4096-row range) reads as `false` (all-disabled). Hot read path."); the kernel already evaluates inverted matches (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/enable/enable_store.rs:376 "SET. `invert == true` (Disabled / `without_enabled`): a row matches iff its"), so a per-tag declared default is a small kernel feature (KF-enable-initial-polarity) with no page allocation. A bitset tag cannot be inserted through a bundle today (boyko_macros/src/component.rs:326 "a bitset tag has no `ComponentPool` and must not be"). The hook route (an on_add hook enabling the bit) needs a deferred enable that the hook command surface does not have: it offers insert / remove / despawn (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/hooks/deferred_master.rs:242 "pub fn insert&lt;B: Bundle>(&mut self, bundle: B) -> &mut Self {"; boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/component/hooks/deferred_master.rs:251 "pub fn remove&lt;C: Component>(&mut self) -> &mut Self {"). ScratchOut&lt;T> is refuted by this form. The LightTableDirty mark the seed sets for new lights must then come from collect_lights' own gate on Added rows; verify when implementing. This row runs every frame (Added&lt;> scan). Class: Runs every frame; an empty Added&lt;> result collects into a zero-capacity Vec (no allocation), so it allocates only on frames that add lights. The heap Vec is forced by the System::Out = Vec&lt;EntityId> contract (light_system.rs:778-780 says so); the destination ScratchColumn already exists (field ids: ScratchColumn&lt;EntityId>, light_system.rs:702) - what is missing is a kernel way for a cached system to write into it. Readers: LightSeedState::collect_added_light_ids -> run_added: EcsMaster::run_cached_system every frame, result extend_from_slice'd into the ScratchColumn ids, then dropped |

| crates/boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:78 `AssetError::Decode(msg.into())` | glb::err (msg.into()) | `String<u8>` | D | diagnostics | none | NEW:InlineStr&lt;N> |  | [local; growth once; addr_cached no; rung R6] ECS: AssetError message read only by the E0801 log call (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/server.rs:171 "boyko_log::codes::E0801,"); its home is the kernel diagnostics substrate as a code + POD args record formatted at the sink (KF-structured-asset-error, replacing boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23 "Decode(String),"), not ECS data. [rev2 GAP 6] ecs_form diagnostics (new explicit form, decided by performance): cold diagnostic text and error payloads, built only on error / report paths, so the cost that matters is I-cache and the emitting thread's time, not allocation speed. Home, chosen per site: a &'static str or structured code (no bytes at all), a boyko_log ring record with formatting deferred to the drain (off the emitting thread), or a cold VmReservation-backed byte arena for a payload that must outlive the call; the recount's memory destination (DspBuf / InlineStr / DiagArena / KF-10 structured error) names which. Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. Not out of scope: our code allocates it. Class: [SUPPLEMENTAL - not a census-pattern hit; an implicit heap allocation found by the second sweep] cold error path. The String becomes the payload of AssetError::Decode(String) (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23), which outlives any decode-scoped arena (it is carried out to the asset server and reported), so a byte arena needs a lifetime it does not have; a fixed inline fmt::Write buffer (truncating, marked) removes the heap entirely. Leaving it on the global allocator would trip the planned #[global_allocator] deny gate on every malformed asset file. The AssetError type itself belongs to the boyko_ecs group. Every err("...") with a &str literal (glb.rs:508, :512, :536, :546, :912, :956) allocates here via Into&lt;String>. It reshapes into a flat Copy tape: node rows {kind, value or str_start, str_len, first_child, next_sibling} in one ScratchColumn, plus string bytes in a ScratchColumn&lt;u8>. The tree is only ever read through get / arr_at lookups. No arena type is needed. Class: load-path decode transient: born and dropped inside one AssetLoader::decode call (loaders run at boot/asset-load, never per frame; the MeshData/TextureData doc anticipates a future threadpool-dispatched decode, rung A5, so the arena must be per-decode and Send-able). Lowest migration priority. Recursive, non-Copy parse tree: the arena must host non-Copy nodes (ArenaStr / ArenaVec views), not VmColumn&lt;T: Copy>. | crates/boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:211 `fn string(&mut self) -> Result<String, String> {` | Parser::string -> Result&lt;String, String> | `String<u8>` | B | system-scratch | system | NEW:LoadArena |  | [return; growth once; addr_cached no; rung R6] ECS: A per-decode transient of a load-path function (boot or asset load, never per frame). component/dense-component: no entity. enable-state: no boolean. relation: no edge. event: produced and consumed inside one call by one pass, not a message between systems. resource-column: nothing in it survives the call. => system-scratch: a ScratchColumn lane of the loader decode context (KF-loader-decode-context), cleared per decode and never freed. owning_entity 'system' is the decode pass. It reshapes into a flat Copy tape: node rows {kind, value or str_start, str_len, first_child, next_sibling} in one ScratchColumn, plus string bytes in a ScratchColumn&lt;u8>. The tree is only ever read through get / arr_at lookups. No arena type is needed. Class: load-path decode transient: born and dropped inside one AssetLoader::decode call (loaders run at boot/asset-load, never per frame; the MeshData/TextureData doc anticipates a future threadpool-dispatched decode, rung A5, so the arena must be per-decode and Send-able). Home, chosen per site: a &'static str or structured code (no bytes at all), a boyko_log ring record with formatting deferred to the drain (off the emitting thread), or a cold VmReservation-backed byte arena for a payload that must outlive the call; the recount's memory destination (DspBuf / InlineStr / DiagArena / KF-10 structured error) names which. | crates/boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:264 `let mut out = Vec::new();` | Parser::array::out | `Vec<Json>` | B | system-scratch | system | NEW:LoadArena |  | [local; growth once; addr_cached no; rung R6] ECS: A per-decode transient of a load-path function (boot or asset load, never per frame). component/dense-component: no entity. enable-state: no boolean. relation: no edge. event: produced and consumed inside one call by one pass, not a message between systems. resource-column: nothing in it survives the call. => system-scratch: a ScratchColumn lane of the loader decode context (KF-loader-decode-context), cleared per decode and never freed. owning_entity 'system' is the decode pass. LoadArena is refuted: the glb JSON tree (boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:90 "enum Json {", with Str(String) / Arr(Vec&lt;Json>) / Obj(Vec&lt;(String, Json)>)) is the one recursive non-Copy structure. It reshapes into a flat Copy tape: node rows {kind, value or str_start, str_len, first_child, next_sibling} in one ScratchColumn, plus string bytes in a ScratchColumn&lt;u8>. The tree is only ever read through get / arr_at lookups. No arena type is needed. Class: load-path decode transient: born and dropped inside one AssetLoader::decode call (loaders run at boot/asset-load, never per frame; the MeshData/TextureData doc anticipates a future threadpool-dispatched decode, rung A5, so the arena must be per-decode and Send-able). Lowest migration priority. Readers: GlbMeshLoader::decode (AssetLoader, load path) -> MeshData -> build_mesh_gpu via the boot-only run_system(upload_mesh_assets) at boyko_app/src/runner.rs:836 |

It lives on the load system's ScratchColumn lanes (KF-09, lanes truncated after the upload drains them), and the handoff record is the `Staged<A::Cpu>` component on the asset entity (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:647 "\| `AssetStaging<A>` \| NonSend Vec \| component `Staged<A::Cpu>` on the asset entity \| AS5 \|") holding a Copy {start, len} span into them; today the upload runs right after load at boot (joltab:crates/boyko_app/src/runner.rs:836 "app.world_mut().run_system(upload_mesh_assets);"). resource-column (rev 2) is withdrawn with the per-type staging resource that Q1 deletes. Decode is synchronous on the caller's thread today (server.rs:127-133 reads, decodes and pushes), so a !Send build view is enough; threadpool decode (rung A5) would add per-worker reserved ranges. This row is the per-primitive output that is appended to the payload after its placement is baked. decode_primitive can write straight into the payload lanes at boyko_render/src/loaders/glb.rs:924 "let base = vertices.len() as u32;" and transform the appended range in place, which removes the per-primitive copy. [rev3 item 2, engine decision Q1 applied] main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DECISIONS.md:23 "\| Q1 \| Assets as entities \| **(a) Assets are entities** \| yes \| A per-draw handle-resolution microbench regressing". The rev-2 skip reason "an asset row is not an entity" is withdrawn: under Q1 every asset is an entity. ecs_form system-scratch: a decode payload that exists only from decode to upload, at load time (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DECISIONS.md:38 "- **Where the cost is.** Structural changes happen at load and unload time, not per frame."). It lives on the load system's ScratchColumn lanes (KF-09, lanes truncated after the upload drains them), and the handoff record is the `Staged<A::Cpu>` component on the asset entity (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:647 "\| `AssetStaging<A>` \| NonSend Vec \| component `Staged<A::Cpu>` on the asset entity \| AS5 \|") holding a Copy {start, len} span into them; today the upload runs right after load at boot (joltab:crates/boyko_app/src/runner.rs:836 "app.world_mut().run_system(upload_mesh_assets);"). resource-column (rev 2) is withdrawn with the per-type staging resource that Q1 deletes. Class: load-path decode transient: born and dropped inside one AssetLoader::decode call (loaders run at boot/asset-load, never per frame; the MeshData/TextureData doc anticipates a future threadpool-dispatched decode, rung A5, so the arena must be per-decode and Send-able). Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. Not out of scope: our code allocates it. Class: cold error path. The String becomes the payload of AssetError::Decode(String) (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23), which outlives any decode-scoped arena (it is carried out to the asset server and reported), so a byte arena needs a lifetime it does not have; a fixed inline fmt::Write buffer (truncating, marked) removes the heap entirely. Leaving it on the global allocator would trip the planned #[global_allocator] deny gate on every malformed asset file. The AssetError type itself belongs to the boyko_ecs group. That is not 'static, so it cannot be a registered column layout; store (u32 start, u32 len) offsets into the line instead, which are Copy and 'static. Class: load-path decode transient: born and dropped inside one AssetLoader::decode call (loaders run at boot/asset-load, never per frame; the MeshData/TextureData doc anticipates a future threadpool-dispatched decode, rung A5, so the arena must be per-decode and Send-able). Lowest migration priority. Allocates once PER NON-EMPTY LINE of the file (the heaviest allocation-count site in the loaders); an arena reset per line or an InlineVec with an n-gon bound would remove the churn. | crates/boyko_render/src/loaders/obj.rs:91 `return Err(decode_error("obj file has no faces".to_owned()));` | ObjMeshLoader::decode (error .to_owned()) | `String<u8 (formatted message)>` | D | diagnostics | none | NEW:InlineStr&lt;N> |  | [local; growth once; addr_cached no; rung R6] ECS: AssetError message read only by the E0801 log call (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/server.rs:171 "boyko_log::codes::E0801,"); its home is the kernel diagnostics substrate as a code + POD args record formatted at the sink (KF-structured-asset-error, replacing boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23 "Decode(String),"), not ECS data. [rev2 GAP 6] ecs_form diagnostics (new explicit form, decided by performance): cold diagnostic text and error payloads, built only on error / report paths, so the cost that matters is I-cache and the emitting thread's time, not allocation speed. Home, chosen per site: a &'static str or structured code (no bytes at all), a boyko_log ring record with formatting deferred to the drain (off the emitting thread), or a cold VmReservation-backed byte arena for a payload that must outlive the call; the recount's memory destination (DspBuf / InlineStr / DiagArena / KF-10 structured error) names which. Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. Not out of scope: our code allocates it. Class: cold error path. The String becomes the payload of AssetError::Decode(String) (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23), which outlives any decode-scoped arena (it is carried out to the asset server and reported), so a byte arena needs a lifetime it does not have; a fixed inline fmt::Write buffer (truncating, marked) removes the heap entirely. Leaving it on the global allocator would trip the planned #[global_allocator] deny gate on every malformed asset file. The AssetError type itself belongs to the boyko_ecs group. It lives on the load system's ScratchColumn lanes (KF-09, lanes truncated after the upload drains them), and the handoff record is the `Staged<A::Cpu>` component on the asset entity (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:647 "\| `AssetStaging<A>` \| NonSend Vec \| component `Staged<A::Cpu>` on the asset entity \| AS5 \|") holding a Copy {start, len} span into them; today the upload runs right after load at boot (joltab:crates/boyko_app/src/runner.rs:836 "app.world_mut().run_system(upload_mesh_assets);"). resource-column (rev 2) is withdrawn with the per-type staging resource that Q1 deletes. | crates/boyko_render/src/loaders/ron_material.rs:110 `fn decode_error(msg: String) -> AssetError {` | ron_material::decode_error(msg: String) | `String<u8>` | D | diagnostics | none | NEW:InlineStr&lt;N> |  | [param; growth once; addr_cached no; rung R6] ECS: AssetError message read only by the E0801 log call (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/server.rs:171 "boyko_log::codes::E0801,"); its home is the kernel diagnostics substrate as a code + POD args record formatted at the sink (KF-structured-asset-error, replacing boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23 "Decode(String),"), not ECS data. [rev2 GAP 6] ecs_form diagnostics (new explicit form, decided by performance): cold diagnostic text and error payloads, built only on error / report paths, so the cost that matters is I-cache and the emitting thread's time, not allocation speed. Home, chosen per site: a &'static str or structured code (no bytes at all), a boyko_log ring record with formatting deferred to the drain (off the emitting thread), or a cold VmReservation-backed byte arena for a payload that must outlive the call; the recount's memory destination (DspBuf / InlineStr / DiagArena / KF-10 structured error) names which. Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. Not out of scope: our code allocates it. Class: cold error path. The String becomes the payload of AssetError::Decode(String) (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/error.rs:23), which outlives any decode-scoped arena (it is carried out to the asset server and reported), so a byte arena needs a lifetime it does not have; a fixed inline fmt::Write buffer (truncating, marked) removes the heap entirely. Leaving it on the global allocator would trip the planned #[global_allocator] deny gate on every malformed asset file. The AssetError type itself belongs to the boyko_ecs group. What is missing is a way to adopt a value that fill() rejected (boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/asset/assets.rs:453 "pub fn fill(&mut self, handle: Handle&lt;T>, value: T) -> Result&lt;(), (AssetError, T)> {") into a fresh Retiring row and get a RetireTicket (kernel feature KF-assets-adopt-retiring). The side queue then disappears; VmQueue is refuted. FINDING: this queue has no producer, and the rejected value is dropped today. The only production fill caller discards the Err (boyko_render/src/gpu_upload.rs:120 "let _ = assets.fill(staged.handle, gpu);"), the drop glue frees no device memory (boyko_render/src/mesh.rs:204 "trivial drop), freeing NO device memory."), and the queue's own doc says boyko_render/src/mesh_assets.rs:729 "(no `fill` caller exists in-tree yet, so this is always `true` today).", which is stale. PLAN CONFLICT: the asset-streaming plan (plan_ref) chose a dedicated !Send Vec queue. The owner's 2026-09-10 order supersedes the Vec, and this ledger routes the value into the existing store instead. [rev3 item 2, engine decision Q1 applied] main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DECISIONS.md:23 "\| Q1 \| Assets as entities \| **(a) Assets are entities** \| yes \| A per-draw handle-resolution microbench regressing". The rev-2 skip reason "an asset row is not an entity" is withdrawn: under Q1 every asset is an entity. ecs_form dense-component: a retired or fill-rejected GPU value stays in its K3 group slot, in the group's `dying` list with its bytes intact, and is freed only by the horizon release K6' (KF-49), whose visitor destroys the device lane in the same pass (main:docs/unification/ENGINE-RUNTIME-ECS-DESIGN.md:589 "\| 14 **(rev 2)** \| orphan queues \| NonSend `Vec<(T,u64)>` \| **K3 deferred release with a horizon** (ED16) (Q1a) / EK17 (Q1b) \| AS2 \|"). The side queue disappears; the value never leaves the asset's own slot. !Send: the device lane of the group is reached only by the dispatcher-solo release pass, as the NonSend store is today. KF-37 stays superseded. Class: Fence-gated device-teardown queue. MeshGpu is NOT Copy (owns !Send device buffers + optional BLAS, no Drop), so VmColumn&lt;T: Copy>/ScratchColumn&lt;T: Copy> cannot hold it - needs an owning, move-out VM queue. [rev2 GAP 6] ecs_form diagnostics (new explicit form, decided by performance): cold diagnostic text and error payloads, built only on error / report paths, so the cost that matters is I-cache and the emitting thread's time, not allocation speed. Home, chosen per site: a &'static str or structured code (no bytes at all), a boyko_log ring record with formatting deferred to the drain (off the emitting thread), or a cold VmReservation-backed byte arena for a payload that must outlive the call; the recount's memory destination (DspBuf / InlineStr / DiagArena / KF-10 structured error) names which. Not kernel-internal: it is not the kernel's storage bookkeeping (most of these rows are in boyko_app and boyko_render), and moving it out lets the gate on kernel-internal close. Not out of scope: our code allocates it. [rev3 item 4] ecs_form resource-column: a numeric capture (not text, not an error payload) retained across frames until the report or dump is written, so the narrowed `diagnostics` form does not fit. Home: a resource-owned kernel column reserved at its bound (commit on touch, no growth reallocation), which is what this row's own note prescribes. Class D keeps it in rung 6. Bucket index is ilog2 of a u64 run length, so at most 64 buckets - a [u64; 64]+len inline array is exact.
